Appendix C
Proof of
Theorem

4.1

Proof.

First, recall that

(199)

\hat{\pi}_{i}=\frac{\sum_{j:j\neq i}\hat{p}_{ji}w_{ji}\hat{\pi}_{j}}{\sum_{j:j\neq i}\hat{p}_{ij}w_{ij}}.

As in the proof of
Theorem

3.1
, we define the auxiliary vector
\bar{\bm{\pi}}
as

(200)

\bar{\pi}_{i}\triangleq\frac{\sum_{j:j\neq i}\hat{p}_{ji}w_{ji}\pi_{j}}{\sum_{j:j\neq i}\hat{p}_{ij}w_{ij}}.

Next, we decompose the entry-wise error as

(201)

\displaystyle\frac{\hat{\pi}_{i}-\pi_{i}}{\pi_{i}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\bar{\pi}_{i}-\pi_{i}}{\pi_{i}}+\frac{\hat{\pi}_{i}-\bar{\pi}_{i}}{\pi_{i}}

(202)

\displaystyle\mathrel{{\mathop{=}\limits}}\frac{\boxed{\textstyle\sum_{j:j\neq i}w_{ij}(\hat{p}_{ji}\pi_{j}-\hat{p}_{ij}\pi_{i})}\ \mathrlap{\hbox to9.4pt{\vbox to9.4pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 4.7pt\lower-4.7pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{{}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 6.23 0 C 6.23 3.44 3.44 6.23 0 6.23 C -3.44 6.23 -6.23 3.44 -6.23 0 C -6.23 -3.44 -3.44 -6.23 0 -6.23 C 3.44 -6.23 6.23 -3.44 6.23 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-2.5pt}{-2.15277pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{62}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
}
\lxSVG@closescope {{{}}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}}{\boxed{\textstyle\pi_{i}\sum_{j:j\neq i}w_{ij}\hat{p}_{ij}}\ \mathrlap{\hbox to9.4pt{\vbox to9.4pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 4.7pt\lower-4.7pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{{}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 6.23 0 C 6.23 3.44 3.44 6.23 0 6.23 C -3.44 6.23 -6.23 3.44 -6.23 0 C -6.23 -3.44 -3.44 -6.23 0 -6.23 C 3.44 -6.23 6.23 -3.44 6.23 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-2.77779pt}{-3.47221pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{62}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
}
\lxSVG@closescope {{{}}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}}+\boxed{\frac{\hat{\pi}_{i}-\bar{\pi}_{i}}{\pi_{i}}}\ \hbox to9.4pt{\vbox to9.4pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 4.7pt\lower-4.7pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{{}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 6.23 0 C 6.23 3.44 3.44 6.23 0 6.23 C -3.44 6.23 -6.23 3.44 -6.23 0 C -6.23 -3.44 -3.44 -6.23 0 -6.23 C 3.44 -6.23 6.23 -3.44 6.23 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-2.22221pt}{-2.15277pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{62}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
}
\lxSVG@closescope {{{}}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}

where
Equation

202
follows from
w_{ij}=w_{ji}
.

First, we upper bound the magnitude of

a

with high probability.

(203)

\displaystyle\phantom{{}={}}\mathbb{P}\left(|\hbox to9.4pt{\vbox to9.4pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 4.7pt\lower-4.7pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{{}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 6.23 0 C 6.23 3.44 3.44 6.23 0 6.23 C -3.44 6.23 -6.23 3.44 -6.23 0 C -6.23 -3.44 -3.44 -6.23 0 -6.23 C 3.44 -6.23 6.23 -3.44 6.23 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-2.5pt}{-2.15277pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{62}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
}
\lxSVG@closescope {{{}}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}|\leq\|\bm{\pi}\|_{\infty}\sqrt{\frac{40n\log(n)p}{k}}\right)

(204)

\displaystyle\mathrel{{\mathop{\geq}\limits}}1-2\exp\left(-\frac{2\left(\|\bm{\pi}\|_{\infty}\sqrt{\frac{40n\log(n)p}{k}}\right)^{2}}{\sum_{j:j\neq i}w_{ij}^{2}(\pi_{i}+\pi_{j})^{2}/k}\right)

(205)

\displaystyle\geq 1-2\exp\left(-\frac{20n\log(n)p}{\sum_{j:j\neq i}w_{ij}^{2}}\right)

(206)

\displaystyle\mathrel{{\mathop{\geq}\limits}}1-2\exp\left(-10\log(n)\right)=1-\frac{2}{n^{10}},

where
Equation

204
is due to Hoeffding’s inequality and
Equation

206
is due to
\sum_{j:j\neq i}w^{2}_{ij}\leq\sum_{j:j\neq i}w_{ij}\leq 2np
.

Next, to lower bound

b

, we first define the
\bm{\pi}
-weighted Laplacian matrix
L_{\bm{\pi}}^{W}
and the corresponding quantity
d_{\text{min}}^{\bm{\pi}}
,

(207)

\displaystyle L_{\bm{\pi}}^{W}

\displaystyle\triangleq\sum_{i<j\leq n}\frac{\pi_{i}\pi_{j}}{\pi_{i}+\pi_{j}}w_{ij}(\bm{e}_{i}-\bm{e}_{j})(\bm{e}_{i}-\bm{e}_{j})^{\mathrm{T}},

(208)

\displaystyle d_{\text{min}}^{\bm{\pi}}

\displaystyle\triangleq\min_{i\in[n]}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\frac{\pi_{i}\pi_{j}}{\pi_{i}+\pi_{j}}w_{ij}.

Note that since

(209)

\frac{\|\bm{\pi}\|_{\infty}}{2h}\leq\frac{\pi_{i}\pi_{j}}{\pi_{i}+\pi_{j}}\leq\frac{\|\bm{\pi}\|_{\infty}}{2},

we have that (cf.
(
Yang et al. 2024
, Section 4.1)
)

(210)

\frac{\|\bm{\pi}\|_{\infty}}{2h}\lambda_{n-1}(L^{W})\leq\lambda_{n-1}(L_{\bm{\pi}}^{W})\leq\frac{\|\bm{\pi}\|_{\infty}}{2}\lambda_{n-1}(L^{W}).

Then,

(211)

b

\displaystyle=\underbracket{\sum_{j=1}^{n}\frac{\pi_{i}\pi_{j}}{\pi_{i}+\pi_{j}}w_{ij}}_{\geq d_{\text{min}}^{\bm{\pi}}}+\underbracket{\pi_{i}\sum_{j=1}^{n}w_{ij}(\hat{p}_{ij}-p_{ij})}_{\hbox to9.4pt{\vbox to9.4pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 4.7pt\lower-4.7pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{{}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 6.23 0 C 6.23 3.44 3.44 6.23 0 6.23 C -3.44 6.23 -6.23 3.44 -6.23 0 C -6.23 -3.44 -3.44 -6.23 0 -6.23 C 3.44 -6.23 6.23 -3.44 6.23 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-2.20834pt}{-2.43054pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{62}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
}
\lxSVG@closescope {{{}}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}},

and

(212)

\displaystyle\phantom{{}={}}\mathbb{P}\left(\hbox to9.4pt{\vbox to9.4pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 4.7pt\lower-4.7pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{{}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 6.23 0 C 6.23 3.44 3.44 6.23 0 6.23 C -3.44 6.23 -6.23 3.44 -6.23 0 C -6.23 -3.44 -3.44 -6.23 0 -6.23 C 3.44 -6.23 6.23 -3.44 6.23 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-2.77779pt}{-3.47221pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{62}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
}
\lxSVG@closescope {{{}}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}\geq-\|\bm{\pi}\|_{\infty}\sqrt{\frac{10n\log(n)p}{k}}\right)

(213)

\displaystyle\geq 1-\exp\left(-\frac{2\left(\|\bm{\pi}\|_{\infty}\sqrt{\frac{10n\log(n)p}{k}}\right)^{2}}{\pi_{i}^{2}\sum_{j=1}^{n}w_{ij}^{2}/k}\right)\geq 1-\frac{1}{n^{10}}

due to Hoeffding’s inequality. Thus, with high probability, we have

(214)

b

\displaystyle\!\!\mathrel{{\mathop{\boxed{\geq}}\limits}}d_{\text{min}}^{\bm{\pi}}-\|\bm{\pi}\|_{\infty}\sqrt{\frac{10n\log(n)p}{k}}

(215)

\displaystyle\mathrel{{\mathop{\geq}\limits}}d_{\text{min}}^{\bm{\pi}}-\|\bm{\pi}\|_{\infty}\sqrt{\frac{10n\log(n)p\lambda_{n-1}(L^{W})^{2}}{640h^{2}n\log(n)p}}

(216)

\displaystyle\mathrel{{\mathop{\geq}\limits}}\frac{\lambda_{n-1}(L_{\bm{\pi}}^{W})}{2}-\frac{\|\bm{\pi}\|_{\infty}\lambda_{n-1}(L^{W})}{8h}

(217)

\displaystyle\mathrel{{\mathop{\geq}\limits}}\frac{\lambda_{n-1}(L_{\bm{\pi}}^{W})}{4}

where
Equation

215
follows from the
k
assumption,
Equation

216
is due to
\lambda_{n-1}(L_{\bm{\pi}}^{W})\leq 2d_{\text{min}}^{\bm{\pi}}
from
(
Yang et al. 2024
, Lemma 18)
, and
Equation

217
follows from
Equation

210
.

Next, we focus on

c

, which we will denote with
\delta_{i}
. Note that

(218)

c

\displaystyle=\delta_{i}=\frac{\hat{\pi}_{i}-\bar{\pi}_{i}}{\pi_{i}}=\frac{\sum_{j}w_{ji}\hat{p}_{ji}(\hat{\pi}_{j}-\pi_{j})}{\pi_{i}\sum_{j}w_{ij}\hat{p}_{ij}}

(219)

\displaystyle=\frac{\sum_{j}w_{ji}\hat{p}_{ji}\pi_{j}\delta_{j}}{\pi_{i}\sum_{j}w_{ij}\hat{p}_{ij}}+\frac{\sum_{j}w_{ji}\hat{p}_{ji}(\bar{\pi}_{j}-\pi_{j})}{\pi_{i}\sum_{j}w_{ij}\hat{p}_{ij}},

thus

(220)

(\pi_{i}\sum_{j}w_{ij}\hat{p}_{ij})\delta_{i}-\sum_{j:j\neq i}w_{ji}\hat{p}_{ji}\pi_{j}\delta_{j}=\sum_{j}w_{ji}\hat{p}_{ji}(\bar{\pi}_{j}-\pi_{j}).

This can be re-expressed as

(221)

\hat{L}_{\bm{\pi}}^{W}\bm{\delta}=\bm{r},

where

(222)

\displaystyle(\hat{L}_{\bm{\pi}}^{W})_{ij}

\displaystyle\triangleq\begin{cases}-w_{ji}\hat{p}_{ji}\pi_{j},&\text{if }i\neq j,\\
\pi_{i}\sum_{k}w_{ik}\hat{p}_{ik},&\text{otherwise},\end{cases}

(223)

\displaystyle r_{i}

\displaystyle\triangleq\sum_{j}w_{ji}\hat{p}_{ji}(\bar{\pi}_{j}-\pi_{j}).

Note that
\mathbb{E}[\hat{L}_{\bm{\pi}}^{W}]=L_{\bm{\pi}}^{W}
and
\hat{L}_{\bm{\pi}}^{W}
is column-stochastic (but not necessarily row-stochastic, unlike
L_{\bm{\pi}}^{W}
). Next, we use Bernstein’s inequality for matrices
(
Tropp 2015
)
to bound
\|\hat{L}_{\bm{\pi}}^{W}-L_{\bm{\pi}}^{W}\|_{2}
with high probability. Let
r_{ijl}
be the
l\in[k]
-th comparison result for the pair
(i,j)
with
r_{ijl}\sim\mathsf{Bernoulli}(p_{ij})
,
r_{jil}=1-r_{ijl}
, and
\sum_{l\in[k]}r_{ijl}/k=\hat{p}_{ij}
. For all
(i,j)\in\mathcal{E}
with
i>j
and
l\in[k]
, define the matrix
X_{jil}
to be

(224)

X_{jil}\triangleq\frac{1}{k}w_{ji}(r_{jil}-p_{ji})(\bm{e}_{j}-\bm{e}_{i})(\pi_{j}\bm{e}_{j}-\pi_{i}\bm{e}_{i})^{\mathrm{T}}.

Observe that
\mathbb{E}[X_{jil}]=0_{n\times n}
,

(225)

\displaystyle\|\mathbb{E}[X_{jil}^{\mathrm{T}}X_{jil}]\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\|\mathbb{E}[X_{jil}X_{jil}^{\mathrm{T}}]\|_{2}=\frac{2}{k^{2}}w_{ji}^{2}p_{ji}(1-p_{ji})(\pi_{i}^{2}+\pi_{j}^{2})

(226)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{w_{ji}^{2}\|\bm{\pi}\|_{\infty}^{2}}{k^{2}},

(227)

\|X_{jil}\|_{2}=\frac{1}{k}w_{ji}|r_{jil}-p_{ji}|\sqrt{2(\pi_{i}^{2}+\pi_{j}^{2})}\leq\frac{2\|\bm{\pi}\|_{\infty}}{k},

and

(228)

\sum_{(i,j)\in\mathcal{E},i>j}\sum_{l\in[k]}X_{jil}=\hat{L}_{\bm{\pi}}^{W}-L_{\bm{\pi}}^{W}.

Thus,

(229)

\displaystyle\phantom{{}={}}\mathbb{P}\left(\|\hat{L}_{\bm{\pi}}^{W}-L_{\bm{\pi}}^{W}\|_{2}\geq\|\bm{\pi}\|_{\infty}\sqrt{\frac{40n^{2}p\log(n)}{k}}\right)

(230)

\displaystyle\leq 2n\exp\left(\frac{-\|\bm{\pi}\|_{\infty}^{2}\frac{40n^{2}p\log(n)}{2k}}{\text{var}(\hat{L}_{\bm{\pi}}^{W}-L_{\bm{\pi}}^{W})+\frac{2\|\bm{\pi}\|_{\infty}^{2}}{3k}\sqrt{\frac{n^{2}p\log(n)}{k}}}\right)

(231)

\displaystyle\leq 2n\exp\left(\frac{-20n^{2}p\log(n)}{n^{2}p+\frac{2}{3}\sqrt{\frac{n^{2}p\log(n)}{k}}}\right)

(232)

\displaystyle\leq 2n\exp(-10\log(n))\leq 2n^{-9}.

Thus, from the assumption on
k
, we have

(233)

\|\hat{L}_{\bm{\pi}}^{W}-L_{\bm{\pi}}^{W}\|_{2}\mathrel{{\mathop{\boxed{\leq}}\limits}}\ \ \frac{h}{n}\sqrt{\frac{40n^{2}p\log(n)}{k}}\leq\frac{\lambda_{n-1}(L_{\bm{\pi}}^{W})}{2}.

Therefore, by taking the
\ell^{2}
norm on both sides of
Equation

221
, we get

(234)

\displaystyle\|\bm{r}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\|\hat{L}_{\bm{\pi}}^{W}\bm{\delta}\|_{2}\geq\|L_{\bm{\pi}}^{W}\bm{\delta}\|_{2}-\|\hat{L}_{\bm{\pi}}^{W}-L_{\bm{\pi}}^{W}\|_{2}\|\bm{\delta}\|_{2}

(235)

\displaystyle\!\!\mathrel{{\mathop{\boxed{\geq}}\limits}}\lambda_{n-1}(L_{\bm{\pi}}^{W})\left(\|\bm{\delta}\|_{2}-\frac{|\bm{\delta}^{\mathrm{T}}\bm{1}_{n}|}{\sqrt{n}}\right)-\frac{\lambda_{n-1}(L_{\bm{\pi}}^{W})}{2}\|\bm{\delta}\|_{2}

(236)

\displaystyle=\lambda_{n-1}(L_{\bm{\pi}}^{W})\left(\frac{\|\bm{\delta}\|_{2}}{2}-\frac{|\bm{\delta}^{\mathrm{T}}\bm{1}_{n}|}{\sqrt{n}}\right),

thus

(237)

\|\bm{\delta}\|_{2}\mathrel{{\mathop{\boxed{\leq}}\limits}}2\left(\frac{\|\bm{r}\|_{2}}{\lambda_{n-1}(L_{\bm{\pi}}^{W})}+\frac{|\bm{\delta}^{\mathrm{T}}\bm{1}_{n}|}{\sqrt{n}}\right).

Next, we bound each element of
\bm{r}
.

(238)

\displaystyle r_{i}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j}w_{ji}\hat{p}_{ji}(\bar{\pi}_{j}-\pi_{j})

(239)

\displaystyle=\underbracket{\sum_{j}w_{ji}\hat{p}_{ji}(\bar{\pi}_{j}-\bar{\pi}_{j}^{(-i)})}_{\hbox to9.4pt{\vbox to9.4pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 4.7pt\lower-4.7pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{{}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 6.23 0 C 6.23 3.44 3.44 6.23 0 6.23 C -3.44 6.23 -6.23 3.44 -6.23 0 C -6.23 -3.44 -3.44 -6.23 0 -6.23 C 3.44 -6.23 6.23 -3.44 6.23 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-2.20834pt}{-2.43054pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{62}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
}
\lxSVG@closescope {{{}}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}+\underbracket{\sum_{j}w_{ji}\hat{p}_{ji}(\bar{\pi}_{j}^{(-i)}-\pi_{j})}_{\hbox to9.4pt{\vbox to9.4pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 4.7pt\lower-4.7pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{{}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 6.23 0 C 6.23 3.44 3.44 6.23 0 6.23 C -3.44 6.23 -6.23 3.44 -6.23 0 C -6.23 -3.44 -3.44 -6.23 0 -6.23 C 3.44 -6.23 6.23 -3.44 6.23 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-1.77779pt}{-1.50694pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{62}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
}
\lxSVG@closescope {{{}}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}},

where
\bar{\pi}_{j}^{(-i)}
is the leave-one-out version of
\bar{\pi}_{j}
,

(240)

\bar{\pi}_{j}^{(-i)}\triangleq\frac{\sum_{l\notin\{i,j\}}w_{lj}\hat{p}_{lj}\pi_{l}+w_{ij}p_{ij}\pi_{i}}{\sum_{l\notin\{i,j\}}w_{jl}\hat{p}_{jl}+w_{ji}p_{ji}}.

First, note the identity

(241)

\frac{\hbox to9.4pt{\vbox to9.4pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 4.7pt\lower-4.7pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{{}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 6.23 0 C 6.23 3.44 3.44 6.23 0 6.23 C -3.44 6.23 -6.23 3.44 -6.23 0 C -6.23 -3.44 -3.44 -6.23 0 -6.23 C 3.44 -6.23 6.23 -3.44 6.23 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-2.5pt}{-3.22221pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{62}\lxSVG@closescope }}}

\displaystyle=\frac{w_{ij}\pi_{i}(p_{ij}-\hat{p}_{ij})}{\sum_{l\notin\{i,j\}}w_{jl}\hat{p}_{jl}+w_{ji}p_{ji}}+\bar{\pi}_{j}\frac{w_{ji}(\hat{p}_{ji}-p_{ji})}{\sum_{l\notin\{i,j\}}w_{jl}\hat{p}_{jl}+w_{ji}p_{ji}}

(246)

\displaystyle\!\!\mathrel{{\mathop{\boxed{\leq}}\limits}}\quad\frac{4w_{ij}\|\bm{\pi}\|_{\infty}^{2}(p_{ij}-\hat{p}_{ij})}{\lambda_{n-1}(L_{\bm{\pi}}^{W})}\left(1+\boxed{\frac{\pi_{j}-\bar{\pi}_{j}}{\pi_{j}}}\right)

(247)

\displaystyle\!\!\mathrel{{\mathop{\boxed{\leq}}\limits}}\quad\frac{4w_{ij}\|\bm{\pi}\|_{\infty}^{2}(p_{ij}-\hat{p}_{ij})}{\lambda_{n-1}(L_{\bm{\pi}}^{W})}\left(1+\frac{4\|\bm{\pi}\|_{\infty}\sqrt{\frac{40n\log(n)p}{k}}}{\lambda_{n-1}(L_{\bm{\pi}}^{W})}\right)

(248)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{16hw_{ij}\|\bm{\pi}\|_{\infty}(p_{ij}-\hat{p}_{ij})}{\lambda_{n-1}(L^{W})},

where
\hbox to9.4pt{\vbox to9.4pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 4.7pt\lower-4.7pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{{}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 6.23 0 C 6.23 3.44 3.44 6.23 0 6.23 C -3.44 6.23 -6.23 3.44 -6.23 0 C -6.23 -3.44 -3.44 -6.23 0 -6.23 C 3.44 -6.23 6.23 -3.44 6.23 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-2.77779pt}{-3.47221pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{62}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
}
\lxSVG@closescope {{{}}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}^{\prime}
is a leave-one-out version of

b

and can be bounded similarly.
Thus,

(249)

d

\displaystyle\!\!\mathrel{{\mathop{\boxed{\leq}}\limits}}\quad\sum_{j}\frac{16hw_{ij}\|\bm{\pi}\|_{\infty}(p_{ij}-\hat{p}_{ij})}{\lambda_{n-1}(L^{W})}

(250)

\displaystyle\!\!\mathrel{{\mathop{\boxed{\leq}}\limits}}\quad\frac{16h\|\bm{\pi}\|_{\infty}\sqrt{\frac{10n\log(n)p}{k}}}{\lambda_{n-1}(L^{W})}.

Next,

(251)

e

\displaystyle=\sum_{j}w_{ji}\hat{p}_{ji}\frac{\sum_{l\notin\{i,j\}}w_{lj}(\hat{p}_{lj}\pi_{l}-\hat{p}_{jl}\pi_{j})}{\sum_{l\notin\{i,j\}}w_{jl}\hat{p}_{jl}+w_{ji}p_{ji}}

(252)

\displaystyle\!\!\mathrel{{\mathop{\boxed{\leq}}\limits}}\frac{4\|\bm{\pi}\|_{\infty}\overbracket{\textstyle\sum_{j}\sum_{l\notin\{i,j\}}w_{ji}w_{lj}(\hat{p}_{lj}\pi_{l}-\hat{p}_{jl}\pi_{j})}^{\hbox to9.4pt{\vbox to9.4pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 4.7pt\lower-4.7pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{{}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 6.23 0 C 6.23 3.44 3.44 6.23 0 6.23 C -3.44 6.23 -6.23 3.44 -6.23 0 C -6.23 -3.44 -3.44 -6.23 0 -6.23 C 3.44 -6.23 6.23 -3.44 6.23 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-1.2396pt}{-2.43054pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{62}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
}
\lxSVG@closescope {{{}}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}}{\lambda_{n-1}(L_{\bm{\pi}}^{W})}.

Using Hoeffding’s inequality,

(253)

\displaystyle\phantom{{}={}}\mathbb{P}\left(|\hbox to9.4pt{\vbox to9.4pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 4.7pt\lower-4.7pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{{}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 6.23 0 C 6.23 3.44 3.44 6.23 0 6.23 C -3.44 6.23 -6.23 3.44 -6.23 0 C -6.23 -3.44 -3.44 -6.23 0 -6.23 C 3.44 -6.23 6.23 -3.44 6.23 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-1.52779pt}{-3.47221pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{62}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
}
\lxSVG@closescope {{{}}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}|\leq\|\bm{\pi}\|_{\infty}np\sqrt{\frac{80\log(n)}{k}}\right)

(254)

\displaystyle\geq 1-2\exp\left(-\frac{2\left(\|\bm{\pi}\|_{\infty}np\sqrt{\frac{80\log(n)}{k}}\right)^{2}}{\sum_{j}\sum_{l\notin\{i,j\}}w_{ji}^{2}w_{lj}^{2}(\pi_{l}+\pi_{j})^{2}/k}\right)

(255)

\displaystyle\geq 1-2\exp\left(-\frac{40n^{2}p^{2}\log(n)}{\sum_{j}\sum_{l\notin\{i,j\}}w_{ji}^{2}w_{lj}^{2}}\right)

(256)

\displaystyle\geq 1-2\exp\left(-10\log(n)\right)=1-\frac{2}{n^{10}},

Thus,

(257)

\hbox to9.4pt{\vbox to9.4pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 4.7pt\lower-4.7pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{{}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 6.23 0 C 6.23 3.44 3.44 6.23 0 6.23 C -3.44 6.23 -6.23 3.44 -6.23 0 C -6.23 -3.44 -3.44 -6.23 0 -6.23 C 3.44 -6.23 6.23 -3.44 6.23 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-2.22221pt}{-2.15277pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{62}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
}
\lxSVG@closescope {{{}}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}\mathrel{{\mathop{\boxed{\leq}}\limits}}\frac{16h\|\bm{\pi}\|_{\infty}np\sqrt{\frac{20\log(n)}{k}}}{\lambda_{n-1}(L^{W})},

which implies

(258)

\displaystyle\|\bm{r}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{n}\|\bm{r}\|_{\infty}

(259)

\displaystyle\!\!\mathrel{{\mathop{\boxed{\leq}}\limits}}\sqrt{n}(1+\sqrt{2np})\frac{16h\|\bm{\pi}\|_{\infty}\sqrt{\frac{10n\log(n)p}{k}}}{\lambda_{n-1}(L^{W})}.

Now we bound
|\bm{\delta}^{\mathrm{T}}\bm{1}_{n}|
.

(260)

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{i}\delta_{i}\right|

\displaystyle\leq hn\left|\sum_{i}\pi_{i}\delta_{i}\right|

(261)

\displaystyle=hn\left|\sum_{i}\hat{\pi}_{i}-\bar{\pi}_{i}\right|

(262)

\displaystyle=hn\left|\sum_{i}\pi_{i}-\bar{\pi}_{i}\right|

(263)

\displaystyle\leq hn\|\bm{\pi}\|_{\infty}\left|\sum_{i}\frac{\sum_{j:j\neq i}w_{ij}(\hat{p}_{ji}\pi_{j}-\hat{p}_{ij}\pi_{i})}{\pi_{i}\sum_{j:j\neq i}w_{ij}\hat{p}_{ij}}\right|

(264)

\displaystyle\!\!\mathrel{{\mathop{\boxed{\leq}}\limits}}2h^{2}n\left|\frac{\overbracket{\textstyle\sum_{i}\sum_{j:j\neq i}w_{ij}(\hat{p}_{ji}\pi_{j}-\hat{p}_{ij}\pi_{i})}^{\hbox to9.4pt{\vbox to9.4pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 4.7pt\lower-4.7pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{{}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 6.23 0 C 6.23 3.44 3.44 6.23 0 6.23 C -3.44 6.23 -6.23 3.44 -6.23 0 C -6.23 -3.44 -3.44 -6.23 0 -6.23 C 3.44 -6.23 6.23 -3.44 6.23 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-1.99306pt}{-0.8264pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{62}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
}
\lxSVG@closescope {{{}}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}}{\lambda_{n-1}(L^{W})}\right|,

and using Hoeffding’s inequality,

(265)

\displaystyle\phantom{{}={}}\mathbb{P}\left(|\hbox to9.4pt{\vbox to9.4pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 4.7pt\lower-4.7pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{{}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 6.23 0 C 6.23 3.44 3.44 6.23 0 6.23 C -3.44 6.23 -6.23 3.44 -6.23 0 C -6.23 -3.44 -3.44 -6.23 0 -6.23 C 3.44 -6.23 6.23 -3.44 6.23 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-2.5pt}{-1.18056pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{62}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
}
\lxSVG@closescope {{{}}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}|\leq\|\bm{\pi}\|_{\infty}n\sqrt{\frac{40\log(n)p}{k}}\right)

(266)

\displaystyle\geq 1-2\exp\left(-\frac{2\left(\|\bm{\pi}\|_{\infty}n\sqrt{\frac{40\log(n)p}{k}}\right)^{2}}{\sum_{i}\sum_{j:j\neq i}w_{ij}^{2}(\pi_{l}+\pi_{j})^{2}/k}\right)

(267)

\displaystyle\geq 1-2\exp\left(-\frac{20n^{2}p\log(n)}{\sum_{i}\sum_{j:j\neq i}w_{ij}^{2}}\right)

(268)

\displaystyle\geq 1-2\exp\left(-10\log(n)\right)=1-\frac{2}{n^{10}},

thus

(269)

|\bm{\delta}^{\mathrm{T}}\bm{1}_{n}|\ \!\!\mathrel{{\mathop{\boxed{\leq}}\limits}}2n^{2}h^{2}\|\bm{\pi}\|_{\infty}\frac{\sqrt{\frac{40\log(n)p}{k}}}{\lambda_{n-1}(L^{W})}.

Therefore,

(270)

\displaystyle\|\bm{\delta}\|_{2}\

\displaystyle\!\!\mathrel{{\mathop{\boxed{\leq}}\limits}}2\left(\frac{\|\bm{r}\|_{2}}{\lambda_{n-1}(L_{\bm{\pi}}^{W})}+\frac{|\bm{\delta}^{\mathrm{T}}\bm{1}_{n}|}{\sqrt{n}}\right)

(271)

\displaystyle\leq 2\left(2h\frac{\|\bm{r}\|_{2}}{\|\bm{\pi}\|_{\infty}\lambda_{n-1}(L^{W})}+\frac{|\bm{\delta}^{\mathrm{T}}\bm{1}_{n}|}{\sqrt{n}}\right)

(272)

\displaystyle\!\!\mathrel{{\mathop{\boxed{\leq}}\limits}}2\Biggl(2h\frac{16h\sqrt{n}(1+\sqrt{2np})\sqrt{\frac{10n\log(n)p}{k}}}{\lambda_{n-1}(L^{W})^{2}}

(273)

\displaystyle\qquad+2nh^{2}\|\bm{\pi}\|_{\infty}\frac{\sqrt{\frac{40n\log(n)p}{k}}}{\lambda_{n-1}(L^{W})}\Biggr)

(274)

\displaystyle\leq 4nh^{2}\Biggl(\frac{32p\sqrt{\frac{20n\log(n)}{k}}}{\lambda_{n-1}(L^{W})^{2}}+\frac{\|\bm{\pi}\|_{\infty}\sqrt{\frac{40n\log(n)p}{k}}}{\lambda_{n-1}(L^{W})}\Biggr).

Next, since

(275)

\displaystyle\delta_{i}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sum_{j}w_{ji}\hat{p}_{ji}\pi_{j}\delta_{j}}{\pi_{i}\sum_{j}w_{ij}\hat{p}_{ij}}+\frac{\sum_{j}w_{ji}\hat{p}_{ji}(\bar{\pi}_{j}-\pi_{j})}{\pi_{i}\sum_{j}w_{ij}\hat{p}_{ij}}

(276)

\displaystyle\!\!\mathrel{{\mathop{\boxed{\leq}}\limits}}\ \ \ \frac{8h}{\|\bm{\pi}\|_{\infty}\lambda_{n-1}(L^{W})}\left(\sqrt{\sum_{j}w_{ji}^{2}\hat{p}_{ji}^{2}\pi_{j}^{2}}\|\bm{\delta}\|_{2}+r_{i}\right)

(277)

\displaystyle\!\!\mathrel{{\mathop{\boxed{\leq}}\limits}}\ \ \frac{8h}{\|\bm{\pi}\|_{\infty}\lambda_{n-1}(L^{W})}\Biggl(

(278)

\displaystyle\qquad\sqrt{np}\|\bm{\pi}\|_{\infty}4nh^{2}\biggl(\frac{32p\sqrt{\frac{20n\log(n)}{k}}}{\lambda_{n-1}(L^{W})^{2}}+\frac{\|\bm{\pi}\|_{\infty}\sqrt{\frac{40n\log(n)p}{k}}}{\lambda_{n-1}(L^{W})}\biggr)

(279)

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{32hnp\|\bm{\pi}\|_{\infty}\sqrt{\frac{20\log(n)}{k}}}{\lambda_{n-1}(L^{W})}\Biggr)

(280)

\displaystyle=\frac{1024n^{2}h^{3}p\sqrt{\frac{20\log(n)p}{k}}}{\lambda_{n-1}(L^{W})^{3}}+\frac{32h^{4}np\sqrt{\frac{40\log(n)}{k}}}{\lambda_{n-1}(L^{W})^{2}}

(281)

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{256h^{2}np\sqrt{\frac{20\log(n)}{k}}}{\lambda_{n-1}(L^{W})^{2}}.

Finally, we have

(282)

\displaystyle\phantom{{}={}}\frac{\|\hat{\bm{\pi}}-\bm{\pi}\|_{\infty}}{\|\bm{\pi}\|_{\infty}}

(283)

\displaystyle\!\!\mathrel{{\mathop{\boxed{\leq}}\limits}}\frac{8h\sqrt{\frac{40n\log(n)p}{k}}}{\lambda_{n-1}(L^{W})}+\|\bm{\delta}\|_{\infty}

(284)

\displaystyle\!\!\mathrel{{\mathop{\boxed{\leq}}\limits}}\ \ \frac{\overbracket{16\sqrt{10}h}^{c_{1}}}{\lambda_{n-1}(L^{W})}\sqrt{\frac{n\log(n)p}{k}}+\frac{\overbracket{2048\sqrt{5}h^{3}}^{c_{3}}}{\lambda_{n-1}(L^{W})^{3}}\sqrt{\frac{n^{4}\log(n)p^{3}}{k}}

(285)

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{\overbracket{64\sqrt{10}h^{4}+512\sqrt{5}h^{2}}^{c_{2}}}{\lambda_{n-1}(L^{W})^{2}}\sqrt{\frac{n^{2}\log(n)p^{2}}{k}},

completing the proof.
∎
